# textdb — stress & comparative test results

Generated by `textdb-bench report`. Values are medians across repetitions; latencies are per operation. `N/A` = operation not available on that backend (reason recorded); **FAIL** = oracle violated.

## Operations — latency by operation

Every timed call a suite made, attributed to a named operation. `p50`/`p99` are the median across tests of each test's percentile; `calls` is the total number of timed calls. A backend that reports no row for an operation never performed one.

## Accuracy checks (untimed)

These run outside every timed span, before and after each cell. They exist so a backend cannot post good latencies for work it did not do. **A cell that fails any check publishes no timings at all** — its latency rows are withheld and counted under `timings voided`.

| check | what it proves | fs | sql-text-sqlite | textdb-sqlite | textdb-sqlite@account | textdb-pg | textdb-pg@account |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| clean_start | store empty before the suite ran | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass |
| canary_roundtrip | create → read → delete round-trip | 64 pass, 2 n/a | 64 pass, 2 n/a | 64 pass, 2 n/a | 64 pass, 2 n/a | 64 pass, 2 n/a | 64 pass, 2 n/a |
| listed_readable | every listed document readable | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass |
| size_consistent | listed size equals bytes read | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass |
| storage_nonzero | documents stored, storage reported | 61 pass | 61 pass | 64 pass | 64 pass | 64 pass | 64 pass |
| oracle_match | every document matches the oracle | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass | 66 pass |
| timings voided | cells whose latencies were withheld | 0 | 0 | **4** | 0 | **1** | **1** |
